# stdaudit mapping (decision 0276 audit, 2026-11-10). Format:
#   go.Symbol = xo name            mapped (name checked against `xo doc`/`xo symbols`)
#   go.Symbol = not planned: why   skipped on purpose
#   go.Symbol = gap: what          a real gap (counts as a gap row in the report)
# The key is the last element of the Go import path, then the symbol (Type.Method for methods).
# Outside Go's standard library (scripts/stdaudit/outside.txt, decision 0655) the key is the import path's last element (go-amqp.Dial)
# (a /vN suffix skipped); PKG.behavior.ITEM = gap: what is a behavior checklist item a symbol diff misses.

sse.behavior.parse = std/sse.Parser (HTML Living Standard 9.2.6: fields, comments, a leading BOM, CR, LF and CRLF line ends, id, retry)
sse.behavior.reconnect = std/sse.Source (Last-Event-ID, the retry field, an Attempt policy)
sse.behavior.server = std/sse.send
sse.behavior.wpt = std/sse_test.xo (the 19 web-platform-tests eventsource format cases on message.py's bodies, whole and byte by byte; 2026-11-15)
go.behavior.spec_surface = std/go (spec/std.md 12: 21 declared names, all present)
go.behavior.errors_is = std/go.Error (a Go error that came from Go goes back as that error)
go.behavior.values = std/go.Any
go.behavior.channels = std/go.Recv
amqptest.behavior.spec_surface = std/amqptest (spec/std.md 64.6: every declared name present)
amqptest.behavior.entities = std/amqptest.Broker (queues, topics, subscriptions, event hubs, sessions, dead letters, scheduled and deferred messages, CBS tokens, failure injection)
amqptest.behavior.rules = std/amqptest.Broker.add_rule (SQL and correlation filters, SQL rule actions, the $management add-rule, remove-rule and enumerate-rules)
deque.behavior.spec_surface = std/deque (spec/std.md 47: every declared name present)
deque.behavior.ring = std/deque.Deque (O(1) at both ends, amortized; container/list's queue use)
brotli.BestCompression = std/brotli.encode (quality: 11)
brotli.BestSpeed = std/brotli.encode (quality: 0)
brotli.DefaultCompression = std/brotli.encode (quality: 6, the default)
brotli.WriterOptions = std/brotli.encode (quality, window: Go's Quality and LGWin)
brotli.NewWriter = std/brotli.compressor (quality 6)
brotli.NewWriterLevel = std/brotli.compressor (quality)
brotli.NewWriterOptions = std/brotli.compressor (quality, window)
brotli.Writer = std/brotli.Compressor
brotli.Writer.Write = std/brotli.Compressor.write
brotli.Writer.Flush = std/brotli.Compressor.flush
brotli.Writer.Close = std/brotli.Compressor.finish
brotli.NewReader = std/brotli.decompressor
brotli.Reader = std/brotli.Decompressor (a checkpoint per meta-block over the straight line decoder, not Go's state machine)
brotli.Reader.Read = std/brotli.Decompressor.write
brotli.HTTPCompressor = std/brotli.responses (http.encoded_responses, decision 0674; wrapped inside http.gzip_responses, br before gzip)
brotli.HTTPCompressorWithLevel = std/brotli.responses (its quality)
brotli.* = not planned: Reset (an encoder or decoder is made per stream); NewWriterV2, Encoder and FastEncoder (the matchfinder package's encoders, a second family with other output; encode follows the reference encoder's bytes)

# go-sqlite3 behavior checklist (decision 0655: what a symbol diff misses; an open item is a gap)
go-sqlite3.behavior.dsn_keys = std/sqlite.config (mattn's _busy_timeout, _journal_mode, _foreign_keys, _synchronous, _txlock, _auto_vacuum, _cache_size, _case_sensitive_like, _defer_foreign_keys, _ignore_check_constraints, _locking_mode, _query_only, _recursive_triggers, _secure_delete, _writable_schema; SQLite's mode= and cache=)
go-sqlite3.behavior.busy_timeout = std/sqlite.driver (busy waits retry in Xo, parked between tries)
go-sqlite3.behavior.time_text = std/sql.Row.time (mattn's SQLiteTimestampFormats, no offset read as UTC, and a DATE, DATETIME or TIMESTAMP column's integer as Unix seconds or milliseconds)
go-sqlite3.behavior.loc = std/sqlite.config (_loc is accepted; mattn parses in UTC and only labels the instant with the zone, and a Time is an instant with no zone, spec/std.md; std/time's Zone gives the local view)
go-sqlite3.behavior.mutex = std/sql.Db (a pooled connection serves one task at a time; opened SQLITE_OPEN_NOMUTEX as _mutex=no)
go-sqlite3.behavior.stmt_cache = std/sql.Db.prepare (prepared statements are explicit; no implicit _stmt_cache_size cache)
go-sqlite3.behavior.vfs = not planned: plug-in extension points: vfs= names a VFS registered from C
go-sqlite3.behavior.read_only_tx = std/sql.Db.begin (a read only transaction runs with PRAGMA query_only)
# protocompile: std/protoparse (with std/protodesc.build for descriptors; bufbuild/protocompile v0.14.1, descriptor shape only, decision 0530)

protocompile.Compiler.Compile = std/protodesc.build (a parsed File and the descriptors of its imports)
protocompile.Resolver = std/protodesc.build (the caller passes the imports' descriptors)
protocompile.ResolverFunc = std/protodesc.build (the caller passes the imports' descriptors)
protocompile.ResolverFunc.FindFileByPath = std/protodesc.build (the caller passes the imports' descriptors)
protocompile.SourceResolver = std/protoparse.gen_set (the caller passes each file's path and parsed source)
protocompile.SourceResolver.FindFileByPath = std/protoparse.gen_set (imports resolve by path within the set)
protocompile.CompositeResolver = std/protoparse.gen_set (imports resolve by path within the set)
protocompile.CompositeResolver.FindFileByPath = std/protoparse.gen_set (imports resolve by path within the set)
protocompile.SearchResult = std/protoparse.File (a parsed source; std/protodesc.FileDesc a built descriptor)
protocompile.SourceAccessorFromMap = std/protoparse.parse (the caller reads the files; parse takes the text)
protocompile.WithStandardImports = std/protoparse.gen_set (google/protobuf's well-known types map to std/protowkt's)
protocompile.PanicError = not planned: Go runtime machinery: a panic recovered inside a compile; Xo's parse returns ParseErr
protocompile.PanicError.Error = not planned: Go runtime machinery: a panic recovered inside a compile; Xo's parse returns ParseErr
protocompile.SourceInfoMode = std/protodesc.build (none) and build_with_source_info (standard; extra_comments and extra_option_locations, alone or both)
protocompile.SourceInfoNone = std/protodesc.build (no SourceCodeInfo, protoc's default without --include_source_info)
protocompile.SourceInfoStandard = std/protodesc.build_with_source_info: protoc's --include_source_info SourceCodeInfo, byte exact on 47 files (std/protodesc_test.xo, scripts/protocdesc)
protocompile.SourceInfoExtraComments = std/protodesc.build_with_source_info: with extra_comments, byte exact with protocompile v0.14.1 (std/protodesc_test.xo)
protocompile.SourceInfoExtraOptionLocations = std/protodesc.build_with_source_info: with extra_option_locations, byte exact with protocompile v0.14.1 (std/protodesc_test.xo)
protocompile.IsEditionSupported = std/protoparse.parse (proto2, proto3 and editions 2023 and 2024, as protoc 36.2)
# protocompile behavior checklist (decision 0655: what a symbol diff misses; an open item is a gap)
protocompile.behavior.proto3 = std/protoparse.parse (proto3: fields, maps, oneofs, optional, services, options; std/protodesc byte exact with protoc 36.2 on 47 files)
protocompile.behavior.proto2 = std/protoparse.parse (labels, required, defaults, groups, extensions ranges, extend)
protocompile.behavior.errors = std/protoparse.ParseErr (line and column of the first error)
protocompile.behavior.codegen = std/protoparse.gen_set (xo proto gen: messages and enums across files, unpacked proto2 repeated numbers, the well-known types)
protocompile.behavior.editions = std/protoparse.parse (edition 2023 and 2024: features options, presence and repeated encoding in gen; protodesc byte exact with protoc on scripts/protocdesc/testdata/editions2023.proto)
protocompile.behavior.groups = gap: derive Proto has no group (delimited) encoding, so gen refuses a proto2 group and an editions DELIMITED field
protocompile.behavior.custom_options = std/protodesc.build: custom options (extensions of descriptor.proto's options messages, scalar, enum, message, aggregate and `(ext).a.b` values) as protoc writes them; test scripts/protocdesc/testdata/customopts.proto
protocompile.behavior.invalid_utf8_strings = std/protoparse.parse (option values and Field.default_value are Bytes: `"\377"` keeps byte 0xFF, as protoc; std/protodesc writes them as protoc does, owner decision #46)
